581: function applyProgramWritePatch08172(target,patch){
582:   if(!target||!patch)return target;
583:   for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(patch)){if(value!==undefined)target[key]=value;}
584:   if(patch.commonReportFormUrl!==undefined)target.reportFormUrl=patch.commonReportFormUrl;
585:   if(patch.activityType!==undefined)target.type=patch.activityType;
586:   if(patch.nightLinked!==undefined)target.affectsAttendance=Boolean(patch.nightLinked);
587:   return target;
588: }

590: async function migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce(){
591:   if(currentRoleId()!=="admin" || state.settings?.programSheetMigrationV07662 || !window.schoolBoard?.saveProgramOperation) return;
592:   const overrides=state.programOverrides&&typeof state.programOverrides==="object"?state.programOverrides:{};
593:   const programs=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.programs)?readonlyCache.programs:[];
594:   let changed=false, failed=false;
595:   for(const [id,override] of Object.entries(overrides)){
596:     if(!override || !["date","time","place","teacher"].some(key=>String(override[key]??"").trim())) continue;
597:     const program=programs.find(item=>String(item.id||"")===String(id));
598:     if(!program?.source) continue;
599:     try{
600:       const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveProgramOperation({role:"admin",source:program.source,programId:program.programId||program.courseId||"",sessionId:program.sessionId||"",date:override.date??program.date??"",time:override.time??program.time??"",place:override.place??program.place??"",teacher:override.teacher??program.teacher??""});
601:       if(!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.reason||"시트 저장 실패");
602:       if(result?.programPatch)applyProgramWritePatch08172(program,result.programPatch);
755:     neisLoading = false;
756:     updateTopSyncStatus();
757:     if (["dashboard", "timetable", "settings"].includes(state.activePage)) render(state.activePage);
758:   }
759: }

947: function closeSensitivePasswordModal(result=null){
948:   const layer=document.getElementById('sensitivePasswordLayer');
949:   if(!layer)return;
950:   const resolver=layer._resolver;
951:   layer.remove();
952:   if(typeof resolver==='function')resolver(result);
953: }
954: function sensitivePasswordModal({mode='unlock',configured=sensitivePasswordConfigured(),kind='sensitive'}={}){
955:   return new Promise(resolve=>{
956:     document.getElementById('sensitivePasswordLayer')?.remove();
957:     const subject=kind==='subject';
958:     const change=mode==='set'&&configured;
959:     const label=subject?'선택과목 대외비':'민감정보';
960:     const layer=document.createElement('div');
961:     layer.id='sensitivePasswordLayer';
962:     layer.className='issue-layer sensitive-password-layer';
963:     layer._resolver=resolve;
964:     const title=mode==='unlock'?`${label} 인증`:(change?`${label} 비밀번호 변경`:`${label} 비밀번호 설정`);
965:     const desc=mode==='unlock'?(subject?'과목별 신청현황을 확인하려면 선택과목 대외비 비밀번호를 입력하세요.':'상세정보를 확인하려면 민감정보 비밀번호를 입력하세요.'):'비밀번호 원문은 저장하지 않고 해시값만 기본정보 연결시트의 공용 설정에 보관합니다.';
966:     layer.innerHTML=`<div class="issue-dialog sensitive-password-dialog">
967:       <header><div><small>${subject?'CONFIDENTIAL · CURRICULUM':'PRIVACY · SECURITY'}</small><h3>🔒 ${title}</h3><p>${desc}</p></div><button type="button" data-sensitive-modal-close>×</button></header>
968:       <div class="sensitive-password-form">
969:         ${change?'<label>현재 비밀번호<input type="password" autocomplete="current-password" data-sensitive-current></label>':''}
970:         <label>${mode==='unlock'?'비밀번호':'새 비밀번호'}<input type="password" autocomplete="${mode==='unlock'?'current-password':'new-password'}" data-sensitive-first></label>
971:         ${mode==='set'?'<label>새 비밀번호 확인<input type="password" autocomplete="new-password" data-sensitive-second></label>':''}
972:         <p class="sensitive-password-error" data-sensitive-error></p>
973:       </div>
974:       <div class="modal-actions"><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-sensitive-modal-close>취소</button><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-sensitive-modal-submit>${mode==='unlock'?'인증':'저장'}</button></div>
975:     </div>`;
976:     document.body.appendChild(layer);
977:     const error=msg=>{const el=layer.querySelector('[data-sensitive-error]');if(el)el.textContent=msg||'';};
978:     const cancel=()=>closeSensitivePasswordModal(null);
979:     const submit=()=>{
980:       const current=layer.querySelector('[data-sensitive-current]')?.value||'';
981:       const first=layer.querySelector('[data-sensitive-first]')?.value||'';
982:       const second=layer.querySelector('[data-sensitive-second]')?.value||'';
983:       if(!first)return error('비밀번호를 입력해 주세요.');
984:       if(mode==='set'&&first.length<4)return error('비밀번호는 4자 이상으로 설정해 주세요.');
985:       if(mode==='set'&&first!==second)return error('새 비밀번호 확인이 일치하지 않습니다.');
986:       closeSensitivePasswordModal({current,first});
987:     };
988:     layer.querySelectorAll('[data-sensitive-modal-close]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=cancel);
989:     layer.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===layer)cancel();};
990:     layer.querySelector('[data-sensitive-modal-submit]').onclick=submit;
991:     const passwordInputs=[...layer.querySelectorAll('input[type="password"]')];
992:     passwordInputs.forEach(input=>{
993:       input.readOnly=false;input.disabled=false;input.inputMode='numeric';input.autocomplete='off';
994:       input.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();submit();return;}event.stopImmediatePropagation();},true);
995:       ['keypress','keyup','beforeinput','input','paste'].forEach(type=>input.addEventListener(type,event=>event.stopImmediatePropagation(),true));
996:     });
997:     setTimeout(()=>layer.querySelector('[data-sensitive-current],[data-sensitive-first]')?.focus(),0);
998:   });
999: }

1151: function applyStudentFocusToPage(page){
1152:   const student=studentFocusStudent(); if(!student)return;
1153:   const classNo=String(student.className||classNumberOf(student)||'').replace(/반/g,'');
1154:   if(page==='scores'){
1155:     scoreMode='combined';
1156:     const scope=$('#scoreScope'),cls=$('#scoreClass'),sel=$('#scoreStudent');
1157:     if(scope)scope.value='individual'; if(cls){cls.value=classNo;cls.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));}
1158:     requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const target=$('#scoreStudent');if(target){target.value=student.id;$('#scoreQueryButton')?.click();}});
1159:   }else if(page==='admissions'){
1160:     const scope=$('#admissionScope'),cls=$('#admissionClass'); if(scope)scope.value='individual';
1161:     if(cls){cls.value=classNo;cls.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));}
1162:     requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const target=$('#admissionStudent');if(target){target.value=student.id;$('#admissionQueryButton')?.click();}});
1163:   }else if(page==='attendance'){
1164:     attendanceStatStudentFilter=attendanceStudentKey(student);
1165:   }else if(page==='records'){
1166:     recordQueryMode='student'; recordClassNo=classNo||recordClassNo; recordStudentId=student.id;
1167:   }
1168: }
1169: function goFromStudentDashboard(page,target=''){
1170:   const student=studentFocusStudent();

1172:   if(page==='admissions')admissionMode=target==='combined'?'combined':(target||'combined');

1176:   // navigate()는 좌측 메뉴 진입 규칙에 따라 생활기록부 첫 탭을 활동내역으로 초기화한다.
1177:   // 학생 대시보드의 선택과목 전체보기에서는 해당 학생·교육과정 탭을 다시 명시하여 흐름을 보존한다.
1178:   if(page==='records'){
1179:     recordMode=target==='curriculum'?'curriculum':target==='final'?'final':'activities';
1180:     recordQueryMode='student';
1181:     if(student){recordStudentId=student.id;recordClassNo=recordStudentClass(student)||recordClassNo;}
1182:     render('records');
1183:   }

2627: function weekdayLabelFromKey(key, long = false) {
2628:   const date = new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
2629:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "";
2630:   const name = ["일","월","화","수","목","금","토"][date.getDay()];
2631:   return long ? `${name}요일` : name;
2632: }
2633: function programStudentGrade(student) {
2634:   const direct = String(student?.grade || student?.schoolGrade || "").match(/[123]/)?.[0];
2635:   if (direct) return direct;
2636:   const studentNo = String(student?.studentNo || student?.number || "").replace(/\.0$/, "").trim();
2637:   if (/^[123]\d{3,}$/.test(studentNo)) return studentNo[0];
2638:   const classText = String(student?.className || student?.class || "");
2639:   const classGrade = classText.match(/^\s*([123])[-\s]/)?.[1];
2640:   return classGrade || "";
2641: }
2642: function timetableClassStyle(label) {
2643:   const text = String(label || "").trim();
2644:   if (!text || text === "-" || !/(?:^|\D)[123]-?\d(?:\D|$)|\d반/.test(text)) return { cls:"", style:"" };
2645:   const palette = [
2646:     ["#e9f6ff","#63a9d8","#174f72"], ["#eef7e8","#78ad61","#315d28"],
2647:     ["#fff2e8","#dd9362","#7a4523"], ["#f3ecff","#9b79d0","#50367a"],
2648:     ["#e8f8f4","#57ab98","#245e52"], ["#fff0f4","#d9829b","#77384b"],
2649:     ["#f7f3df","#b8a75b","#665a21"], ["#eaf0ff","#718ed2","#354c86"],
2650:     ["#f0f4f7","#879caf","#425568"]
2651:   ];
2652:   let hash = 0; for (const ch of text) hash = (hash * 31 + ch.charCodeAt(0)) >>> 0;
2653:   const [fill,border,color] = palette[hash % palette.length];
2654:   return { cls:"class-colored", style:` style="--class-fill:${fill};--class-border:${border};--class-text:${color}"` };
2655: }
2656: function timetableSubjectStyle(subject) {
2657:   const text=String(subject||"").replace(/\s+/g,"");
2658:   const rules=[
2659:     [/국어|한문/,"subject-korean"],[/수학/,"subject-math"],[/영어/,"subject-english"],
2660:     [/사회|한국사|역사|지리|윤리/,"subject-social"],[/과학|물리|화학|생명|지구/,"subject-science"],
2661:     [/진로|동아리|자율|창체/,"subject-creative"],[/체육|음악|미술/,"subject-art"]
2662:   ];
2663:   return rules.find(([pattern])=>pattern.test(text))?.[1]||"subject-other";
2664: }
2789: }
2790: function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
2791:   let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
2792:   const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;
2793:   for(let i=0;i<62;i++){
2794:     d.setDate(d.getDate()+step);
2795:     const key=dateKey(d);
2796:     if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
2797:   }
2798:   return day;
2799: }

2964:       const current = byStudent.get(key) || { ...row, recordCount:0, totalMinutes:0, studentKey:key };
2965:       current.recordCount += 1; current.totalMinutes += Number(row.minutes || 0);
2966:       if (!current.date || String(row.date) >= String(current.date)) Object.assign(current, row);
2967:       current.cumulativeCount = Math.max(Number(current.cumulativeCount || 0), Number(row.cumulativeCount || 0), current.recordCount);
2968:       current.cumulativeMinutes = Math.max(Number(current.cumulativeMinutes || 0), current.totalMinutes);

4547: function accessAccountRole(account){
4548:   if(String(account?.admin||'').toUpperCase()==='Y')return '관리자';
4549:   const role=String(account?.role||'').trim();
4550:   if(/학년부장/.test(role))return '학년부장';
4551:   if(/학년총무/.test(role))return '학년총무';
4552:   if(/담임/.test(role))return '담임교사';
4553:   if(/교감/.test(role))return '교감';
4554:   if(/교장/.test(role))return '교장';
4555:   return '교과담당';
4556: }

5158: function fiveGradeBoundaryEnrollment() {
5159:   const internal = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.scoreType === "내신");
5160:   const counts = new Map();
5161:   internal.forEach(row => {
5162:     const value = Number(String(row?.enrollment ?? "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""));
5163:     if (Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0) counts.set(value, (counts.get(value) || 0) + 1);
5164:   });
5165:   const frequent = [...counts.entries()].sort((a,b) => b[1]-a[1] || b[0]-a[0])[0]?.[0];
5166:   return frequent || (readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(student => !/전출|자퇴|퇴학/.test(String(student.status || "재학"))).length || 259;
5167: }
5168: function fiveGradeBoundaryData(enrollment) {
5169:   const total = Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(enrollment) || 1));
5170:   const cumulativeRates = [0.10, 0.34, 0.66, 0.90, 1];
5171:   const cumulative = cumulativeRates.map(rate => Math.round(total * rate));
5172:   cumulative[cumulative.length - 1] = total;
5173:   const counts = cumulative.map((value, index) => value - (index ? cumulative[index - 1] : 0));
5174:   return { total, cumulative, counts };
5175: }
5176: function fiveGradeBoundaryMarkup() {
5177:   if (scoreMode === "mock") return "";
5178:   const data = fiveGradeBoundaryData(fiveGradeBoundaryEnrollment());
5179:   const grades = data.counts.map((count, index) => `<span><small>${index + 1}등급</small><b>${count}명</b><em>${index ? data.cumulative[index - 1] + 1 : 1}~${data.cumulative[index]}등</em></span>`).join("");
5180:   return `<details class="five-grade-boundary"><summary><div><b>5등급 경계 보정</b><span>수강자 ${data.total}명 기준 · 1등급 ${data.cumulative[0]}등까지</span></div><em>기준 보기</em></summary><div class="five-grade-boundary-body"><div class="five-grade-boundary-control"><label>수강자 수<input id="fiveGradeEnrollment" type="number" min="1" max="999" value="${data.total}"></label><button class="btn secondary" id="fiveGradeApply" type="button">경계 다시 계산</button><p><b>나이스 등급을 본 정보로 사용</b><span>아래 경계표는 수강자 수에 따른 확인용 보조정보입니다. 과목별 수강자 수가 다르면 해당 인원으로 다시 계산하세요.</span></p></div><div class="five-grade-boundary-grid" id="fiveGradeBoundaryGrid">${grades}</div><small>누적 비율 10%·34%·66%·90%에 수강자 수를 곱한 뒤 반올림하여 경계를 산출합니다.</small></div></details>`;
5181: }

7143: function studentAlertLabel(category){return {report:"보고서",selection:"선택과목",attendance:"출결",schedule:"프로그램 일정"}[category]||"학생안내";}
7144: function buildStudentAlertMessage(group,formUrl=""){
7145:   const rows=group?.rows||[], names=rows.slice(0,60).map(row=>`${row.studentNo||""} ${row.name||""}`.trim()).filter(Boolean);
7146:   const link=String(formUrl||group?.reportFormUrl||"").trim();
7147:   if(group.category==="report")return `[운호고 학생 보고서 제출 안내]\n\n${group.title} 활동에 참여한 학생 중 보고서를 아직 제출하지 않은 학생은 보고서를 제출하기 바랍니다. 제출한 내용은 활동의 과정과 성장 내용을 확인하는 생활기록부 작성 근거로 활용됩니다.${link?`\n\n보고서 제출 링크\n${link}`:""}\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`;
7148:   if(group.category==="selection")return `[운호고 선택과목 수정 제출 안내]\n\n선택과목 신청 내역에서 확인이 필요한 학생입니다. 안내받은 오류 내용을 확인한 뒤 구글폼으로 정확하게 다시 제출해 주기 바랍니다. 이후 제출한 최신 내역을 최종 신청으로 반영합니다.\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`;
7149:   if(group.category==="attendance")return `[운호고 출결 확인 안내]\n\n오늘 출결 또는 프로그램 참여 상태에서 확인이 필요한 학생입니다. 해당 내용을 확인하고 필요한 경우 담임교사에게 알려 주기 바랍니다.\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`;
7150:   return `[운호고 프로그램 일정 안내]\n\n${group.title} 프로그램이 ${group.date||"예정일"}에 운영됩니다. 참여 학생은 시간·장소·준비사항을 확인해 주기 바랍니다.${rows.length?`\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`:""}`;
7151: }

7572: function formatNeisYmd(text) {
7573:   const value = String(text || "");
7574:   return /^\d{8}$/.test(value) ? `${Number(value.slice(4, 6))}/${Number(value.slice(6, 8))}` : value;
7575: }

7582: function neisDateToLocalDate(value) {
7583:   const text=String(value||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
7584:   if(!/^\d{8}$/.test(text))return null;
7585:   const date=new Date(Number(text.slice(0,4)),Number(text.slice(4,6))-1,Number(text.slice(6,8)),12,0,0);
7586:   return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())?null:date;
7587: }
7588: function expectedSchoolPeriods(value){
7589:   const date=value instanceof Date?value:neisDateToLocalDate(value);
7590:   const weekdayNo=date?.getDay();
7591:   return weekdayNo===3?5:([1,2,4,5].includes(weekdayNo)?7:0);
7592: }

7723: function normalizeTeacherTimetableCell(cell){
7724:   if(!cell)return null;
7725:   if(typeof cell==="string")return {subject:cell,room:"",changed:false};
7726:   if(Array.isArray(cell)){
7727:     const subject=String(cell[1]||cell[0]||"").trim();
7728:     const room=String(cell[3]||cell[2]||"").trim();
7729:     return subject?{subject,room,changed:false}:null;
7730:   }
7731:   const subject=String(cell.subject||cell.subjectName||cell.name||cell.course||cell.className||"").trim();
7732:   const room=String(cell.location||cell.room||cell.classroom||cell.gradeClass||cell.classNo||"").trim();
7792:   if(masterNames.length)return [...new Set(masterNames)].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,"ko"));
7793:   const names = [...DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS.map(item=>item.person), ...SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA.homeroom.flatMap(row=>[row[2],row[3]])]
7794:     .map(value=>String(value||"").trim())
7795:     .filter(value=>value && !/순회|지도자|담당교사/.test(value));
7796:   return [...new Set(names)].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,"ko"));
7797: }

7812: function normalizedSchoolHours(){
7813:   const defaults=baseState.settings.schoolHours;
7814:   const current=Array.isArray(state.settings.schoolHours)?state.settings.schoolHours:[];
7815:   const byLabel=new Map(current.filter(row=>Array.isArray(row)&&row[0]&&row[0]!=="쉬는시간").map(row=>[String(row[0]),row]));
7816:   const rows=defaults.map(def=>byLabel.get(String(def[0]))||structuredClone(def));
7817:   state.settings.schoolHours=rows;
7818:   return rows;
7819: }
7820: function schoolHourRange(label){
7821:   const row=normalizedSchoolHours().find(item=>String(item[0])===String(label));
7822:   return row?`${row[1]}~${row[2]}`:"";
7823: }

8252: function timetableClassColorKey(room){
8253:   const match=String(room||"").match(/(?:^|\b)([123])-?(\d{1,2})(?:\b|$)/);
8254:   if(!match)return "";
8255:   return `${match[1]}-${Number(match[2])}`;
8256: }
8257: function timetableClassColorClass(room){
8258:   const key=timetableClassColorKey(room);
8259:   if(!key)return "";
8260:   let hash=0; for(const ch of key)hash=(hash*31+ch.charCodeAt(0))>>>0;
8261:   return `class-color-${hash%9}`;
8262: }

8267: function dashboardTimetableAutoMode(){
8268:   if(dashboardTimetableMode)return dashboardTimetableMode;
8269:   const now=new Date(),minute=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes();
8270:   const end=(now.getDay()===3)?(14*60+30):(16*60+30);
8271:   return minute>=end?"night":"day";
8272: }

8386: function dashboardPersonalWeekMarkup(){
8387:   const {week,days}=personalTimetableData();
8388:   const status=schoolPeriodStatus(new Date());
8389:   const todayIndex=Math.max(0,Math.min(4,(today.getDay()+6)%7));
8390:   const maxPeriods=Math.max(7,Array.isArray(week)?week.length:0);
8391:   if(!Array.isArray(week)||!week.some(row=>Array.isArray(row)&&row.some(Boolean))){
8392:     return `<div class="uep-week-timetable-empty"><span>◷</span><b>내 주간시간표 연결 준비</b><small>컴시간 교사시간표가 연결되면 월~금 수업이 이곳에 표시됩니다.</small><button data-page="timetable">시간표 연결 확인</button></div>`;
8393:   }
8394:   const heads=(days?.length?days:["월","화","수","목","금"]).slice(0,5);
8395:   const cells=[];
8396:   for(let period=1;period<=maxPeriods;period+=1){
8397:     cells.push(`<div class="uep-week-period"><b>${period}</b><small>${escapeHtml(periodTimeLabel(period)||"")}</small></div>`);
8398:     for(let d=0;d<5;d+=1){
8399:       const raw=week[period-1]?.[d]||"";
8400:       const [subject,room,flag]=String(raw).split("|");
8401:       const current=d===todayIndex&&status.index===period-1;
8402:       const classColor=timetableClassColorClass(room);
8403:       cells.push(`<button class="uep-week-class personal-order ${raw?"has-class":"is-empty"} ${classColor} ${current?"is-current":""} ${flag==="changed"?"is-changed":""}" data-page="timetable"><b>${escapeHtml(room||"-")}</b><small>${escapeHtml(subject||"")}</small>${current?'<i aria-label="현재 교시"></i>':''}${flag==="changed"?'<em>변동</em>':''}</button>`);
8404:     }
8405:   }
8406:   return `<div class="uep-week-timetable"><div class="uep-week-corner">교시</div>${heads.map((day,i)=>`<div class="uep-week-head ${i===todayIndex?"today":""}"><b>${escapeHtml(String(day).replace(/요일/g,""))}</b></div>`).join("")}${cells.join("")}</div>`;
8407: }

9519: function escapeHtml(value = "") {
9520:   return String(value).replace(/[&<>'"]/g, (ch) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", "'": "&#39;", '"': "&quot;" })[ch]);

9723: function bindExtensionReference() {
9724:   const imageButton = $("[data-extension-tab='image']");
9725:   const tableButton = $("[data-extension-tab='table']");
9726:   const imagePanel = $("#extensionImagePanel");
9727:   const tablePanel = $("#extensionTablePanel");
9728:   const switchTab = (name) => {
9729:     imageButton?.classList.toggle("active", name === "image");
9730:     tableButton?.classList.toggle("active", name === "table");
9731:     imagePanel?.classList.toggle("hidden", name !== "image");
9732:     tablePanel?.classList.toggle("hidden", name !== "table");
9733:   };
9734:   if (imageButton) imageButton.onclick = () => switchTab("image");
9735:   if (tableButton) tableButton.onclick = () => switchTab("table");
9736:   const image = $("#extensionLayoutImage");
9737:   if (image) image.onclick = () => { const zoomed = image.classList.toggle("zoomed"); image.closest(".extension-image-scroll")?.classList.toggle("zoom-mode", zoomed); };
9738:   const search = $("#sourceReferenceSearch");
9739:   if (search) search.oninput = () => {
9740:     const query = search.value.trim().toLowerCase();
9741:     const filtered = SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA.extension.filter((row) => row.join(" ").toLowerCase().includes(query));
9742:     $("#sourceReferenceRows").innerHTML = sourceReferenceRowsMarkup(filtered, "extension");
9743:   };
9744: }
9784:     $("#approvalList").querySelectorAll("[data-approval-index]").forEach((button) => button.onclick = () => showApprovalDetail(button.dataset.approvalIndex));
9785:     showApprovalDetail(0);
9786:   } else if (type === "extension") {
9787:     $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<section id="extensionImagePanel" class="extension-image-panel extension-image-only"><div class="extension-image-scroll"><img id="extensionLayoutImage" src="./assets/extension-layout.png" alt="운호고등학교 인터넷전화 내선번호 자리배치도"></div></section>`;
9788:     $("#drawer").classList.add("extension-expanded");
9789:     bindExtensionReference();
9790:   } else {
9791:     $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="reference-editor-tools source-reference-tools"><input id="sourceReferenceSearch" placeholder="학년·반·담임·부담임 검색"><span>${rows.length}건</span></div><div id="sourceReferenceRows">${sourceReferenceRowsMarkup(rows, type)}</div><p class="drawer-note">2026년 담임교사 명단(부담임 포함) 기준 · 읽기 전용</p>`;
9792:     $("#drawer").classList.add("reference-expanded");
9793:     const bindHomeroomIfNeeded=()=>{if(type==="homeroom")bindHomeroomTeacherLinks();};
9794:     bindHomeroomIfNeeded();
9795:     $("#sourceReferenceSearch").oninput = () => {
9796:       const query = $("#sourceReferenceSearch").value.trim().toLowerCase();

9798:       $("#sourceReferenceRows").innerHTML = sourceReferenceRowsMarkup(filtered, type);
9799:       bindHomeroomIfNeeded();
9800:     };
9801:   }
9802:   $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
9803:   $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
9804: }

9806: function sourceReferenceRowsMarkup(rows, type) {
9807:   if (!rows.length) return '<div class="outing-empty">검색 결과가 없습니다.</div>';
9808:   if (type === "homeroom") {
9809:     const grades = [...new Set(rows.map((row) => String(row[0])))].sort();
9810:     const gradeHeads = { "1": "홍석민", "2": "윤용철", "3": "정조일" };
9811:     return `<div class="homeroom-grade-sections">${grades.map((grade) => {
9812:       const gradeRows = rows.filter((row) => String(row[0]) === grade);
9813:       return `<section class="homeroom-grade-section"><header><div><span>${escapeHtml(grade)}학년</span><b>학년부장 ${escapeHtml(gradeHeads[grade] || "-")}</b></div><small>${gradeRows.length}개 학급</small></header><div class="source-homeroom-grid">${gradeRows.map((row)=>`<div><small>${escapeHtml(row[1])}반</small><button class="homeroom-teacher-link primary" data-homeroom-teacher="${escapeHtml(row[2])}">${escapeHtml(row[2])}</button><span>부담임 <button class="homeroom-teacher-link" data-homeroom-teacher="${escapeHtml(row[3])}">${escapeHtml(row[3])}</button></span></div>`).join("")}</div></section>`;
9814:     }).join("")}</div>`;
9815:   }
9816:   if (type === "extension") {
9817:     return `<div class="source-extension-table"><div class="source-reference-head"><b>구역</b><b>이름·시설</b><b>내선</b><b>전화번호</b></div>${rows.map((row)=>`<div><small>${escapeHtml(row[0] || "-")}</small><b>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "-")}</b><em>${escapeHtml(row[2] || "-")}</em><span>${escapeHtml(row[3] || "-")}</span></div>`).join("")}</div>`;
9818:   }
9819:   return `<div class="source-approval-list">${rows.map((row)=>`<div><b>${escapeHtml(row[0])}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "")}</span>${row[2] ? `<small>${escapeHtml(row[2])}</small>` : ""}</div>`).join("")}</div>`;
9820: }

9833:   $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");

9844:       : '<small class="state warn">읽을 수 있는 행이 없습니다.</small>';
9845:   };
9846:   $("#referenceSmartImport").onsubmit = async (event) => {
9847:     event.preventDefault();

9849:     if (!imported.length) return toast("가져올 표 자료가 없습니다.");
9850:     const mode = document.querySelector('input[name="referenceMode"]:checked')?.value;
9851:     state.settings.referenceData[type] = mode === "append" ? [...rows, ...imported] : imported;
9852:     await save();

9854:     openEditableReference(type);

9858: function referenceRowsMarkup(rows, type = "") {
9859:   if (!rows.length) return '<div class="reference-detail"><div class="outing-empty">등록된 자료가 없습니다. 표 전체 편집에서 원본 표를 붙여넣으세요.</div></div>';
9860:   if (type === "homeroom") {
9861:     const grades = [...new Set(rows.map((row) => String(row[0])))].sort();
9862:     return `<div class="homeroom-grade-sections">${grades.map((grade) => {
9863:       const gradeRows = rows.filter((row) => String(row[0]) === grade);
9864:       return `<section class="homeroom-grade-section"><header><span>${escapeHtml(grade)}학년</span><small>${gradeRows.length}개 학급</small></header><div class="reference-table reference-homeroom">${gradeRows.map((row) => `<div><b>${escapeHtml(row[1])}반</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[2] || "-")}</span><small>부담임 ${escapeHtml(row[3] || "-")}</small></div>`).join("")}</div></section>`;
9865:     }).join("")}</div>`;
9866:   }
9867:   if (type === "extension") {
9868:     return `<div class="reference-table reference-extension">${rows.map((row) => `<div><small>${escapeHtml(row[0] || "기타")}</small><b>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "-")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[2] || "")}</span><em>${escapeHtml(row[3] || "")}</em></div>`).join("")}</div>`;
9869:   }
9870:   return `<div class="reference-detail">${rows.map((row) => `<div class="reference-row reference-approval"><b>${escapeHtml(row[0])}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "")}</span>${row[2] ? `<small>${escapeHtml(row[2])}</small>` : ""}</div>`).join("")}</div>`;
9871: }

9904: async function openMessenger() {
9905:   const currentPath = state.settings.links?.messengerPath || "";
9906:   if (currentPath && window.schoolBoard?.launchExecutable) {
9907:     const result = await window.schoolBoard.launchExecutable(currentPath);
9908:     if (result?.ok) return toast("충북소통메신저를 실행했습니다.");
9909:   }
9910:   $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "CHUNGBUK MESSENGER";
9911:   $("#drawerTitle").textContent = "충북소통메신저 실행 연결";
9912:   $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="connection-guide"><span>↗</span><h3>설치된 메신저 파일을 한 번만 지정하세요</h3><p>${currentPath ? escapeHtml(currentPath) : "아직 실행 파일이 설정되지 않았습니다."}</p><button class="btn primary" id="selectMessengerExecutable">실행 파일 선택</button><small>다음부터 빠른 열기를 누르면 곧바로 실행됩니다. 로그인 정보는 앱에 저장하지 않습니다.</small></div>`;
9913:   $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
9914:   $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");

9916:     const selected = await window.schoolBoard?.selectExecutable?.();

9921:     closeDrawer();

9923:   };

10126: async function selectGoogleCredential() {
10127:   if (!window.schoolBoard?.selectGoogleCredential)
10128:     return openOAuthGuide();
10129:   const result = await window.schoolBoard.selectGoogleCredential();
10130:   if (result?.canceled) return;
10131:   if (!result?.ok) { googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "Google 서비스 계정 인증을 연결하지 못했습니다."; updateTopSyncStatus(); return toast(googleConnectionError); }
10132:   googleConnectionError = "";
10133:   googleConnectionStatus = await window.schoolBoard.googleCredentialStatus();
10134:   const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
10135:   if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
10136:   else googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
10137:   updateTopSyncStatus();
10138:   startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
10139:   toast(`${result.account || "Google 서비스 계정"}으로 UEP 구글시트에 연결했습니다.`);
10140:   render("settings");
10141: }

10143: async function previewReadonlySync() {
10144:   if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync)return toast("학교 공용 데이터 연결 기능을 찾지 못했습니다.");
10145:   if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
10146:     const status=await ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}).catch(()=>null);
10147:     googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status?.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status?.endpointReady!==false};
10148:   }
10149:   if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return toast("학교 공용 읽기 연결에 실패했습니다. 로그아웃 후 이름·이메일로 다시 로그인해 주세요.");
10150:   const button = $("[data-readonly-sync]");
10151:   if (button) {
10152:     button.disabled = true;
10153:     button.textContent = "읽는 중…";
10154:   }
10155:   let result=null;
10156:   try{result=await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();uepDiagRecord('sync.preview.result',result);}
10157:   catch(error){
10158:     uepDiagRecord('sync.preview.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});
10159:     result={ok:false,reason:error?.message||'학교 공용 자료 조회 예외'};
10160:   }
10161:   if (!result?.ok) {
10162:     if (button) {
10163:       button.disabled = false;
10164:       button.textContent = "지금 새로고침";
10165:     }
10166:     googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "동기화하지 못했습니다.";
10167:     updateTopSyncStatus();
10168:     return toast(googleConnectionError);
10169:   }
10170:   googleConnectionError = "";
10171:   updateTopSyncStatus();
10172:   const p = result.preview;

10188: async function retryGoogleConnection() {
10189:   if (!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync) return navigate("settings");
10190:   toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 읽는 중입니다.");

10192:   if (!result?.ok) {
10193:     googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "UEP 구글시트 연결에 실패했습니다.";
10194:     updateTopSyncStatus();
10195:     toast(googleConnectionError);
10196:     render("dashboard");
10197:     return;
10198:   }
10199:   const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
10200:   if (cached?.ok) {
10201:     readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
10202:     googleConnectionError = "";
10203:     toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 불러왔습니다.");
10204:   } else {
10205:     googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
10206:     toast(googleConnectionError);
10207:   }
10208:   updateTopSyncStatus();
10209:   render("dashboard");
10210: }

10236: function timetableWeekLabel(value) {
10237:   const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
10238:   if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return "주간 선택";
10239:   const day = base.getDay();
10240:   base.setDate(base.getDate() + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day));
10241:   const end = new Date(base); end.setDate(end.getDate() + 4);
10242:   const md = d => `${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}`;
10243:   return `${md(base)} ~ ${md(end)}`;
10244: }

10295: function studentActivityOverview(student) {
10296:   const matches=(item)=>String(item?.studentId||"")===String(student.id||"") || (student.studentNo && String(item?.studentNo||item?.number||"")===String(student.studentNo));
10297:   const connected=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).map(programWithOverride).filter(program=>studentRecordWithinEnrollment(program,student)&&(program.students||[]).some(matches));
10298:   const local=(state.programs||[]).filter(program=>studentRecordWithinEnrollment(program,student)&&(program.students||[]).some(matches));
10299:   const programs=[...connected,...local].filter((program,index,array)=>array.findIndex(item=>`${item.id}|${item.date}|${item.title}`===`${program.id}|${program.date}|${program.title}`)===index).sort((a,b)=>String(b.date||"").localeCompare(String(a.date||"")));
10300:   const activities=(readonlyCache?.studentActivities||[]).filter(item=>matches(item)&&studentRecordWithinEnrollment(item,student));
10301:   const text=activities.map(item=>`${item.area} ${item.title} ${item.type} ${item.keywords}`).join(" ");
10302:   const officer=/임원|회장|부회장|실장|부실장|반장|부반장/.test(text);
10303:   const volunteer=/봉사/.test(text);
10304:   const confirmedPrograms=programs.filter(program=>{const participant=(program.students||[]).find(matches);return participant && /출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사/.test(String(participant.result||"대상"));});
10305:   return {programs,activities,officer,volunteer,confirmedPrograms,latest:programs.slice(0,3).map(program=>program.actualTitle||program.title).filter(Boolean)};
10306: }
10307: function studentAttendanceDetails(student){

10309:   return {
10310:     late:(readonlyCache?.lateAttendance||[]).filter(match),
10311:     night:(readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||[]).filter(match),
10312:     official:(readonlyCache?.officialAttendance||[]).filter(match),
10313:     programAbsent:(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).map(programWithOverride).filter(p=>studentRecordWithinEnrollment(p,student)).flatMap(p=>(p.students||[]).filter(x=>studentMatches(x,student)).filter(x=>/결석|불참|미참여/.test(String(x.result||''))).map(x=>({...x,programTitle:p.actualTitle||p.title,date:p.date})))
10314:   };
10315: }

10382: function programStableKey(program){

11897: function openWidgetSettings(key) {
11898:   const labels = {
11899:     weather: "시계·날씨",
11900:     programs: "오늘의 프로그램",
11901:     calendar: "학교 캘린더",

11903:     operations: "오늘의 업무·공지",
11904:     alerts: "지금 확인",
11905:   };
11906:   const setting = state.settings.widgetSettings[key] || {
11907:     visible: true,
11908:     width: "auto",
11909:     fontScale: 100,
11910:   };
11911:   $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "WIDGET SETTINGS";

11913:   $("#drawerBody").innerHTML =
11914:     `<form id="widgetSettingsForm" class="widget-settings-form"><div class="widget-setting-grid"><label>표시 여부<select id="widgetVisible"><option value="true" ${setting.visible !== false ? "selected" : ""}>표시</option><option value="false" ${setting.visible === false ? "selected" : ""}>숨김</option></select></label><label>너비<select id="widgetWidth"><option value="auto" ${setting.width === "auto" ? "selected" : ""}>자동</option><option value="wide" ${setting.width === "wide" ? "selected" : ""}>넓게</option><option value="fixed" ${setting.width === "fixed" ? "selected" : ""}>고정</option></select></label><label>글자 크기<div class="font-stepper"><button type="button" data-font-minus>−</button><output id="widgetFontScale">${setting.fontScale || 100}%</output><button type="button" data-font-plus>＋</button></div></label></div>${key === "timetable" ? `<div class="alert-settings"><label><input type="checkbox" id="showPeriodTimes" ${setting.showTimes !== false ? "checked" : ""}> 교시별 시간 표시</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="startPeriodAlert" ${setting.startAlert ? "checked" : ""}> 수업 시작 알림</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="endPeriodAlert" ${setting.endAlert ? "checked" : ""}> 수업 종료 알림</label></div>` : ""}<button class="btn primary">로컬 설정 저장</button><p class="drawer-note">위젯 설정은 이 PC에만 저장됩니다. 데이터 원본은 수정하지 않습니다.</p></form>`;
11915:   $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
11916:   $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
11917:   let scale = setting.fontScale || 100;
11918:   const updateScale = () => ($("#widgetFontScale").textContent = `${scale}%`);
11919:   $("[data-font-minus]").onclick = () => {
11920:     scale = Math.max(80, scale - 10);
11921:     updateScale();
11922:   };
11923:   $("[data-font-plus]").onclick = () => {
11924:     scale = Math.min(130, scale + 10);
11925:     updateScale();
11926:   };
11927:   $("#widgetSettingsForm").onsubmit = async (event) => {
11928:     event.preventDefault();
11929:     state.settings.widgetSettings[key] = {
11930:       ...setting,
11931:       visible: $("#widgetVisible").value === "true",
11932:       width: $("#widgetWidth").value,
11933:       fontScale: scale,
11934:       ...(key === "timetable"
11935:         ? {
11936:             showTimes: $("#showPeriodTimes").checked,
11937:             startAlert: $("#startPeriodAlert").checked,
11938:             endAlert: $("#endPeriodAlert").checked,
11939:           }
11940:         : {}),
11941:     };
11942:     if (key === "timetable" && (state.settings.widgetSettings[key].startAlert || state.settings.widgetSettings[key].endAlert)) {
11943:       try { if ("Notification" in window && Notification.permission === "default") await Notification.requestPermission(); } catch {}
11944:     }
11945:     await save();
11946:     closeDrawer();
11947:     render(state.activePage);
11948:     toast("위젯 설정을 저장했습니다.");
11949:   };
11950: }

11965: function programRosterTabs(students) {
11966:   const counts={1:0,2:0,3:0}; students.forEach(student=>{const grade=programStudentGrade(student);if(counts[grade]!=null)counts[grade]+=1;});
11967:   return `<div class="program-roster-filter"><div><button class="active" data-program-grade="all">전체 ${students.length}명</button>${[1,2,3].map(grade=>`<button data-program-grade="${grade}" ${counts[grade]?"":"disabled"}>${grade}학년 ${counts[grade]}명</button>`).join("")}</div><span id="programRosterVisibleCount">${students.length}명 표시</span></div>`;
11968: }
11969: function openNightProgramSummary(id, studentKey="", day="", slot=""){
11970:   const all=[...afterSchoolProgramGroups(),...groupedAfterSchoolCourses(afterSchoolProgramGroups()),...(readonlyCache?.programs||[])];

11975:   const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(x=>attendanceStudentKey(x)===String(studentKey)||String(x.id)===String(studentKey)||String(x.studentNo)===String(studentKey));

11984: function normalizeAfterAttendanceStatus(value){
11985:   const text=String(value||"").trim();
11986:   if(/불참\(인정\)|공결|공식 ?행사|인정/.test(text))return "불참(인정)";
11987:   if(/불참\(조퇴\)|조퇴/.test(text))return "불참(조퇴)";
11988:   if(/불참\(결석\)|병결|결석/.test(text))return "불참(결석)";
11989:   if(/불참\(미인정\)|미인정|무단|불참/.test(text))return "불참(미인정)";
11990:   if(/수강|출석|참여|정상|입실|지각/.test(text))return "수강";
11991:   return "신청";
11992: }

12111: function exportReportExcel(report,student){
12112:   const responses=(report.responses||[]); const rows=[["프로그램명",report.programName||""],["학생",`${report.studentNo||student?.studentNo||""} ${report.name||student?.name||""}`],["구분",report.kind||""],["최종제출일(차수)",reportSubmissionLabel(report)],["매칭상태",report.matchStatus||""],[],["문항","응답"],...responses.map((value,index)=>[report.questions?.[index]||reportQuestionLabel(index),value||""])];
12113:   const table=`<table>${rows.map(row=>`<tr>${row.map(cell=>`<td>${xmlEscape(cell)}</td>`).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</table>`; const blob=new Blob([`\ufeff<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"></head><body>${table}</body></html>`],{type:"application/vnd.ms-excel;charset=utf-8"}); const link=document.createElement("a"); link.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob); link.download=`${safeFileName(`${report.studentNo||student?.studentNo||""}_${report.programName||"학생보고서"}`)}.xls`; link.click(); setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(link.href),1000);
12114: }

12336: function parseStudentLine(line) {
12337:   const trimmed = String(line || "").trim();
12338:   if (!trimmed) return { number: "", name: "" };
12339:   const parts = trimmed.includes("\t")
12340:     ? trimmed.split(/\t+/)
12341:     : trimmed.includes(",")
12342:       ? trimmed.split(/,+/)
12343:       : trimmed.split(/\s+/);
12344:   return { number: (parts.shift() || "").trim(), name: parts.join(" ").trim() };
12345: }

12371:   return normalized;
12372: }

12561: function openDormOutingDrawer() {
12562:   const viewKey = dateKey(outingViewDate);
12563:   const liveOutings = (readonlyCache?.dormOutings || []).filter(item=>!dormOutingExcludedByRule(item));
12564:   const items = liveOutings.filter((item) => item.date === viewKey);
12565:   const monday = new Date(outingViewDate);
12566:   const day = monday.getDay();
12567:   monday.setDate(monday.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
12568:   const sunday = new Date(monday);
12569:   sunday.setDate(sunday.getDate() + 6);
12570:   const weekItems = liveOutings.filter((item) => item.date >= dateKey(monday) && item.date <= dateKey(sunday));
12571:   const displayDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ko-KR", { year: "numeric", month: "long", day: "numeric", weekday: "short" }).format(outingViewDate);
12572:   $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "DORMITORY · OUTING";
12573:   $("#drawerTitle").textContent = `학사외출 · ${displayDate}`;
12574:   const outingMasterStudent = (item) => {
12575:     const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
12576:     const ids=[item.studentId,item.studentNo,item.studentNumber].filter(Boolean).map(String);
12577:     const byId=students.find(student=>ids.some(id=>id===String(student.id||"")||id===String(student.studentId||"")||id===String(student.studentNo||"")));
12578:     if(byId)return byId;
12579:     const name=String(item.name||"").trim();
12580:     const cls=String(item.className||item.class||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
12581:     const no=String(item.number||item.no||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
12582:     return students.find(student=>String(student.name||"").trim()===name && (!cls||String(student.className||"").replace(/\D/g,"")===cls) && (!no||String(student.number||"").replace(/\D/g,"")===no)) || null;
12583:   };

12587:     if (/^[123]\d{3}$/.test(masterNo)) return masterNo;

12589:     if (/^[123]\d{3}$/.test(direct)) return direct;

12603:   }).join("");

12610:     const byTime=(getter)=>(a,b)=>String(getter(a)||'99:99').localeCompare(String(getter(b)||'99:99'))||byStudent(a,b);

15016:  async function check(){removeDuplicate();const pill=versionPill();if(!pill)return;try{const r=await fetch('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hkhsm00-k81c/UEP-Updates/main/uep-policy.json?ts='+Date.now(),{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)return;const p=await r.json();const latest=String(p.latestVersion||'').replace(/^v/i,'');if(newer(latest,CURRENT)){pill.textContent='v'+CURRENT+' → v'+latest;pill.classList.add('uep-version-update-available');pill.title='새 업데이트 '+latest+' 사용 가능';}else{pill.textContent='v'+CURRENT;pill.classList.remove('uep-version-update-available');}}catch(e){pill.textContent='v'+CURRENT;}}

15051: function uep08224TypeBadges(detail,type){
15052:   const text=[detail['세부유형'],detail['대표평가구조'],detail['수능최저경향'],detail['담임상담체크'],type&&type['전형유형']].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
15053:   const out=[];
15054:   if(/지역균형|지역인재/.test(text))out.push(/지역인재/.test(text)?'지역인재':'지역균형');
15055:   if(/추천|학교장/.test(text))out.push('추천');
15056:   if(/면접/.test(text))out.push('면접');
15057:   if(/서류|정성/.test(text))out.push('서류');
15058:   if(/논술/.test(text))out.push('논술');
15059:   const min=String(detail['수능최저경향']||'');
15060:   if(/미적용|없음/.test(min))out.push('최저 X'); else if(min)out.push('최저 O');
15061:   return [...new Set(out)].slice(0,5).map(v=>'<span>'+escapeHtml(v)+'</span>').join('');
15062: }
